Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.TypeSafeDispatcher.process(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/TypeSafeDispatcher.java
Original range: 120-285
Documentation:

Method code:
120 |   public T process(AstNode node) {
121 |     switch (node.getType()) {
122 |       case Token.ADD:
123 |       case Token.AND:
124 |       case Token.BITAND:
125 |       case Token.BITOR:
126 |       case Token.BITXOR:
127 |       case Token.COMMA:
128 |       case Token.DIV:
129 |       case Token.EQ:
130 |       case Token.GE:
131 |       case Token.GT:
132 |       case Token.IN:
133 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
134 |       case Token.LE:
135 |       case Token.LSH:
136 |       case Token.LT:
137 |       case Token.MOD:
138 |       case Token.MUL:
139 |       case Token.NE:
140 |       case Token.OR:
141 |       case Token.RSH:
142 |       case Token.SHEQ:
143 |       case Token.SHNE:
144 |       case Token.SUB:
145 |       case Token.URSH:
146 |         return processInfixExpression((InfixExpression) node);
147 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
148 |         return processArrayLiteral((ArrayLiteral) node);
149 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
150 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
159 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
160 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
161 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
162 |       case Token.BITNOT:
163 |       case Token.DEC:
164 |       case Token.DELPROP:
165 |       case Token.INC:
166 |       case Token.NEG:
167 |       case Token.NOT:
168 |       case Token.POS:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
172 |       case Token.BLOCK:
173 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
174 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
175 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
176 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
177 |         } else {
178 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
179 |                                           node.getClass() +
180 |                                           " type: " +
181 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
182 |         }
183 |       case Token.BREAK:
184 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
185 |       case Token.CALL:
186 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
189 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
190 |       case Token.CATCH:
191 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
192 |       case Token.COLON:
193 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
194 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
195 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
196 |       case Token.DO:
197 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
198 |       case Token.EMPTY:
199 |         return (node instanceof EmptyExpression) ?
200 |             processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node) :
201 |             processEmptyStatement((EmptyStatement) node);
202 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
203 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
204 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
205 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
206 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
207 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
208 |         } else {
209 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
210 |                                           node.getClass() +
211 |                                           " type: " +
212 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
213 |         }
214 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
215 |       case Token.FALSE:
216 |       case Token.NULL:
217 |       case Token.THIS:
218 |       case Token.TRUE:
219 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
220 |       case Token.FOR:
221 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
222 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
223 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
224 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
225 |         } else {
226 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
227 |                                           node.getClass() +
228 |                                           " type: " +
229 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
230 |         }
231 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
232 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
233 |       case Token.GETELEM:
234 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
235 |       case Token.GETPROP:
236 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
237 |       case Token.HOOK:
238 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
239 |       case Token.IF:
240 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
241 |       case Token.LABEL:
242 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
243 |       case Token.LP:
244 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NAME:
246 |         return processName((Name) node);
247 |       case Token.NEW:
248 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
249 |       case Token.NUMBER:
250 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
252 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
253 |       case Token.REGEXP:
254 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
255 |       case Token.RETURN:
256 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
257 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
258 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
259 |       case Token.STRING:
260 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
261 |       case Token.SWITCH:
262 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.THROW:
264 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
265 |       case Token.TRY:
266 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
267 |       case Token.CONST:
268 |       case Token.VAR:
269 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
270 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
271 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
272 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
273 |         } else {
274 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
275 |                                           node.getClass() +
276 |                                           " type: " +
277 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
278 |         }
279 |       case Token.WHILE:
280 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
281 |       case Token.WITH:
282 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
283 |     }
284 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
285 |   }

2046 |     return parserConfig;

68 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {

Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a Scope given the parent Scope and the root node of the scope.
   * @param parent  The parent Scope. Cannot be null.
   * @param rootNode  Typically the FUNCTION node.
   */
Method code:
391 |   Scope(Scope parent, Node rootNode) {
392 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(parent);
393 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(rootNode != parent.rootNode);
394 | 
395 |     this.parent = parent;
396 |     this.rootNode = rootNode;
397 |     JSType nodeType = rootNode.getJSType();
398 |     if (nodeType != null && nodeType.isFunctionType()) {
399 |       thisType = nodeType.toMaybeFunctionType().getTypeOfThis();
400 |     } else {
401 |       thisType = parent.thisType;
402 |     }
403 |     this.isBottom = false;
404 |     this.depth = parent.depth + 1;
405 |   }

/* OPT there's a noticeable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
379 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments) {
380 |         final String defaultResource
381 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
382 | 
383 |         Locale locale = Locale.getDefault();
384 | 
385 |         // ResourceBundle does caching.
386 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
387 | 
388 |         String formatString;
389 |         try {
390 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
391 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
392 |             throw new RuntimeException
393 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
394 |         }
395 | 
396 |         /*
397 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
398 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
399 |          * single 's.
400 |          */
401 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
402 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
403 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
404 |     }

   * Generates JavaScript source code for an AST.
   */
Method code:
1792 |   private String toSource(Node n, SourceMap sourceMap, boolean firstOutput) {
1793 |     CodePrinter.Builder builder = new CodePrinter.Builder(n);
1794 |     builder.setPrettyPrint(options.prettyPrint);
1795 |     builder.setLineBreak(options.lineBreak);
1796 |     builder.setPreferLineBreakAtEndOfFile(options.preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile);
1797 |     builder.setSourceMap(sourceMap);

1799 |     builder.setTagAsStrict(firstOutput &&
1800 |         options.getLanguageOut() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT);
1801 |     builder.setLineLengthThreshold(options.lineLengthThreshold);
1802 | 
1803 |     Charset charset = options.outputCharset != null ?
1804 |         Charset.forName(options.outputCharset) : null;
1805 |     builder.setOutputCharset(charset);
1806 | 
1807 |     return builder.build();
1808 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any
   *         object in this object's implicit prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
484 |   final boolean isImplicitPrototype(ObjectType prototype) {
485 |     for (ObjectType current = this;
486 |          current != null;
487 |          current = current.getImplicitPrototype()) {
488 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {
489 |         return true;
490 |       }
491 |     }
492 |     return false;
493 |   }

===== M0047 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.unrollBinaryOperator(Node,int,String,Context,Context,int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 741-757
Documentation:
/**
   * We could use addList recursively here, but sometimes we produce
   * very deeply nested operators and run out of stack space, so we
   * just unroll the recursion when possible.
   *
   * We assume nodes are left-recursive.
   */
Method code:
741 |   private void unrollBinaryOperator(
742 |       Node n, int op, String opStr, Context context,
743 |       Context rhsContext, int leftPrecedence, int rightPrecedence) {
744 |     Node firstNonOperator = n.getFirstChild();
745 |     while (firstNonOperator.getType() == op) {
746 |       firstNonOperator = firstNonOperator.getFirstChild();
747 |     }
748 | 
749 |     addExpr(firstNonOperator, leftPrecedence, context);
750 | 
751 |     Node current = firstNonOperator;
752 |     do {
753 |       current = current.getParent();
754 |       cc.addOp(opStr, true);
755 |       addExpr(current.getFirstChild().getNext(), rightPrecedence, rhsContext);
756 |     } while (current != n);
757 |   }

===== M0062 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 407-434
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
407 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
408 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
409 | 
410 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
411 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
412 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
413 |       return 0;
414 |     }
415 | 
416 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
417 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
418 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
419 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
420 |       // have been added
421 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
422 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
423 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
424 |       }
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
428 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
429 |       return 0;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
433 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
434 |   }

===== M0066 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseVariableDeclarations.GatherCollapses.canBeRedeclared(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseVariableDeclarations.java
Original range: 190-205
Documentation:

Method code:
190 |     private boolean canBeRedeclared(Node n, Scope s) {
191 |       if (!NodeUtil.isExprAssign(n)) {
192 |         return false;
193 |       }
194 |       Node assign = n.getFirstChild();
195 |       Node lhs = assign.getFirstChild();
196 | 
197 |       if (!lhs.isName()) {
198 |         return false;
199 |       }
200 | 
201 |       Var var = s.getVar(lhs.getString());
202 |       return var != null
203 |           && var.getScope() == s
204 |           && !blacklistedVars.contains(var);
205 |     }

===== M0077 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.hoistExterns(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1407-1444
Documentation:
/**
   * Hoists inputs with the @externs annotation into the externs list.
   */
Method code:
1407 |   private void hoistExterns(Node externsRoot) {
1408 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1409 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1410 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement() &&
1411 |           options.closurePass) {
1412 |         // If we're doing scanning dependency info anyway, use that
1413 |         // information to skip sources that obviously aren't externs.
1414 |         if (!input.getProvides().isEmpty() || !input.getRequires().isEmpty()) {
1415 |           continue;
1416 |         }
1417 |       }
1418 | 
1419 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1420 | 
1421 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1422 |       if (n == null) {
1423 |         continue;
1424 |       }
1425 | 
1426 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1427 |       if (info != null && info.isExterns()) {
1428 |         // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1429 |         // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1430 |         // the externs pile anyways.
1431 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1432 |         input.setIsExtern(true);
1433 | 
1434 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1435 | 
1436 |         externs.add(input);
1437 |         staleInputs = true;
1438 |       }
1439 |     }
1440 | 
1441 |     if (staleInputs) {
1442 |       repartitionInputs();
1443 |     }
1444 |   }

===== M0091 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 785-798
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
785 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
786 |     if (item == null) {
787 |       return null;
788 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
789 |       return item.getNext();
790 |     } else {
791 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
792 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
793 |         return item.chain(result);
794 |       } else {
795 |         return item;
796 |       }
797 |     }
798 |   }

===== M0092 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodePrinter.CompactCodePrinter.maybeLineBreak()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 463-483
Documentation:

Method code:
463 |     @Override
464 |     void maybeLineBreak() {
465 |       if (lineBreak) {
466 |         if (sawFunction) {
467 |           startNewLine();
468 |           sawFunction = false;
469 |         }
470 |       }
471 | 
472 |       // Since we are at a legal line break, can we upgrade the
473 |       // preferred break position?  We prefer to break after a
474 |       // semicolon rather than before it.
475 |       int len = code.length();
476 |       if (preferredBreakPosition == len - 1) {
477 |         char ch = code.charAt(len - 1);
478 |         if (ch == ';') {
479 |           preferredBreakPosition = len;
480 |         }
481 |       }
482 |       maybeCutLine();
483 |     }

Documentation:

Method code:
388 |   private void validateVar(Node n) {
389 |     validateNodeType(Token.VAR, n);
390 |     this.validateMinimumChildCount(n, 1);
391 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
392 |       // Don't use the validateName here as the NAME is allowed to have
393 |       // a child.
394 |       validateNodeType(Token.NAME, c);
395 |       validateNonEmptyString(c);
396 |       validateMaximumChildCount(c, 1);
397 |       if (c.hasChildren()) {
398 |         validateExpression(c.getFirstChild());
399 |       }
400 |     }
401 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 381-411
Documentation:
/**
   * Initializes the instance state needed for a compile job if the sources
   * are in modules.
   */
Method code:
381 |   public <T extends SourceFile> void initModules(
382 |       List<T> externs, List<JSModule> modules, CompilerOptions options) {
383 |     initOptions(options);
384 | 
385 |     checkFirstModule(modules);
386 |     fillEmptyModules(modules);
387 | 
388 |     this.externs = makeCompilerInput(externs, true);
389 | 
390 |     // Generate the module graph, and report any errors in the module
391 |     // specification as errors.
392 |     this.modules = modules;
393 |     if (modules.size() > 1) {
394 |       try {
395 |         this.moduleGraph = new JSModuleGraph(modules);
396 |       } catch (JSModuleGraph.ModuleDependenceException e) {
397 |         // problems with the module format.  Report as an error.  The
398 |         // message gives all details.
399 |         report(JSError.make(MODULE_DEPENDENCY_ERROR,
400 |                 e.getModule().getName(), e.getDependentModule().getName()));
401 |         return;
402 |       }
403 |     } else {
404 |       this.moduleGraph = null;
405 |     }
406 | 
407 |     this.inputs = getAllInputsFromModules(modules);
408 |     initBasedOnOptions();
409 | 
410 |     initInputsByIdMap();
411 |   }

===== M0102 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

/** Gets the current scope. */
Method code:
596 |   public Scope getScope() {
597 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
598 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {
599 |       return scope;
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     Iterator<Node> it = scopeRoots.descendingIterator();
603 |     while (it.hasNext()) {
604 |       scope = scopeCreator.createScope(it.next(), scope);
605 |       scopes.push(scope);
606 |     }
607 |     scopeRoots.clear();
608 | 
609 |     return scope;
610 |   }

===== M0136 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

===== M0148 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.createParameters(boolean,JSType...)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 1246-1257
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list.
   *
   * @param lastVarArgs whether the last type should considered as a variable
   *     length argument.
   * @param parameterTypes the parameter types. The last element of this array
   *     is considered a variable length argument is {@code lastVarArgs} is
   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1246 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1247 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1248 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1249 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1250 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1251 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1252 |       } else {
1253 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1254 |       }
1255 |     }
1256 |     return builder.build();
1257 |   }

===== M0175 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseVariableDeclarations.GatherCollapses.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseVariableDeclarations.java
Original range: 136-179
Documentation:

Method code:
136 |     @Override
137 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
138 |       if (n.isVar()) {
139 |         blacklistStubVars(t, n);
140 |       }
141 | 
142 |       // Only care about var nodes
143 |       if (!n.isVar() && !canBeRedeclared(n, t.getScope())) return;
144 | 
145 |       // If we've already looked at this node, skip it
146 |       if (nodesToCollapse.contains(n)) return;
147 | 
148 |       // Adjacent VAR children of an IF node are the if and else parts and can't
149 |       // be collapsed
150 |       if (parent.isIf()) return;
151 | 
152 |       Node varNode = n;
153 | 
154 |       boolean hasVar = n.isVar();
155 | 
156 |       // Find variable declarations that follow this one (if any)
157 |       n = n.getNext();
158 | 
159 |       boolean hasNodesToCollapse = false;
160 | 
161 |       while (n != null &&
162 |           (n.isVar() || canBeRedeclared(n, t.getScope()))) {
163 | 
164 |         if (n.isVar()) {
165 |           blacklistStubVars(t, n);
166 |           hasVar = true;
167 |         }
168 | 
169 |         nodesToCollapse.add(n);
170 |         hasNodesToCollapse = true;
171 | 
172 |         n = n.getNext();
173 |       }
174 | 
175 |       if (hasNodesToCollapse && hasVar) {
176 |         nodesToCollapse.add(varNode);
177 |         collapses.add(new Collapse(varNode, n, parent));
178 |       }
179 |     }

   * Hoists inputs with the @nocompiler annotation out of the inputs.
   */
Method code:
1449 |   private void hoistNoCompileFiles() {
1450 |     boolean staleInputs = false;
1451 |     for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1452 |       Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1453 | 
1454 |       // Inputs can have a null AST on a parse error.
1455 |       if (n == null) {
1456 |         continue;
1457 |       }
1458 |

1460 |       if (info != null && info.isNoCompile()) {
1461 |         input.getModule().remove(input);
1462 |         staleInputs = true;
1463 |       }
1464 |     }
1465 | 
1466 |     if (staleInputs) {
1467 |       repartitionInputs();
1468 |     }

   * equivalent to {@code (ObjectType) getPropertyType("prototype")}.

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 221-242
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
221 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
222 |     if (isAllType) {
223 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
224 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
225 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       } else {
228 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
229 |       }
230 |     } else {
231 |       int size = alternates.size();
232 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
233 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
234 |       } else if (size > 1) {
235 |         return null;
236 |       } else if (size == 1) {
237 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
238 |       } else {
239 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
240 |       }
241 |     }
242 |   }

===== M0187 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.initOptions(CompilerOptions)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 274-337
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
274 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
275 |     this.options = options;
276 |     if (errorManager == null) {
277 |       if (outStream == null) {
278 |         setErrorManager(
279 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
280 |       } else {
281 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
282 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
283 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
284 |         setErrorManager(printer);
285 |       }
286 |     }
287 | 
288 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
289 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
290 |       options.checkTypes = true;
291 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
292 |       options.checkTypes = false;
293 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
294 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
295 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
296 |       // parser type warnings.
297 |       options.setWarningLevel(
298 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
299 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
300 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
301 |     }
302 | 
303 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
304 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
305 |       options.setWarningLevel(
306 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
307 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
308 |     }
309 | 
310 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
311 |       options.setWarningLevel(
312 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
313 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
314 |     }
315 | 
316 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
317 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
318 |     guards.add(
319 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
320 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
321 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
322 | 
323 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
324 | 
325 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
326 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
327 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
328 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
329 |     // error off.
330 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
331 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
332 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
333 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
334 |     }
335 | 
336 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
337 |   }

===== M0200 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1489-1538
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1489 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1490 |     if (type != node.getType()
1491 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1492 |         || this.getClass() != node.getClass()) {
1493 |       return false;
1494 |     }
1495 | 
1496 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1497 |       return false;
1498 |     }
1499 | 
1500 |     if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1501 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1502 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1503 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1504 |         return false;
1505 |       }
1506 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING || type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1507 |       if (type == Token.STRING_KEY) {
1508 |         int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1509 |         int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1510 |         if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1511 |           return false;
1512 |         }
1513 |       }
1514 | 
1515 |       int slashV1 = this.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1516 |       int slashV2 = node.getIntProp(SLASH_V);
1517 |       if (slashV1 != slashV2) {
1518 |         return false;
1519 |       }
1520 |     } else if (type == Token.CALL) {
1521 |       if (this.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL) != node.getBooleanProp(FREE_CALL)) {
1522 |         return false;
1523 |       }
1524 |     }
1525 | 
1526 |     if (recurse) {
1527 |       Node n, n2;
1528 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1529 |            n != null;
1530 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1531 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1532 |           return false;
1533 |         }
1534 |       }
1535 |     }
1536 | 
1537 |     return true;
1538 |   }

     * This may start a new line if the current line is longer than the line
     * length threshold.
     */
Method code:
489 |     @Override
490 |     void maybeCutLine() {
491 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold) {
492 |         // Use the preferred position provided it will break the line.
493 |         if (preferredBreakPosition > lineStartPosition &&
494 |             preferredBreakPosition < lineStartPosition + lineLength) {
495 |           int position = preferredBreakPosition;
496 |           code.insert(position, '\n');
497 |           prevCutPosition = position;
498 |           reportLineCut(lineIndex, position - lineStartPosition, true);
499 |           lineIndex++;
500 |           lineLength -= (position - lineStartPosition);
501 |           lineStartPosition = position + 1;
502 |         } else {
503 |           startNewLine();
504 |         }
505 |       }
506 |     }

===== M0228 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1106-1134
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1106 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1107 |     // unknown
1108 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1109 |       return true;
1110 |     }
1111 |     // all type
1112 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1113 |       return true;
1114 |     }
1115 |     // equality
1116 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1117 |       return true;
1118 |     }
1119 |     // unions
1120 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1121 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1122 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1123 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1124 |           return true;
1125 |         }
1126 |       }
1127 |     }
1128 |     // proxy types
1129 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1130 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1131 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1132 |     }
1133 |     return false;
1134 |   }

===== M0233 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 470-485
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * ECMA-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
470 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
471 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
472 |       Set<String> directives = null;
473 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
474 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
475 |         if (directives == null) {
476 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
477 |         } else {
478 |           directives.add(directive);
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 | 
482 |       if (directives != null) {
483 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
484 |       }
485 |     }

===== M0262 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 504-524
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
504 |   @Override
505 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
506 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
507 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
508 |     if (unknown) {
509 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
510 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
511 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
512 |         unknown = false;
513 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
514 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
515 |             unknown = true;
516 |             break;
517 |           }
518 |         }
519 |       } else {
520 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
521 |       }
522 |     }
523 |     return unknown;
524 |   }

Method code:
233 |   @Override
234 |   boolean defineProperty(String name, JSType type, boolean inferred,
235 |       Node propertyNode) {
236 |     if (hasOwnDeclaredProperty(name)) {
237 |       return false;
238 |     }
239 |     Property newProp = new Property(
240 |         name, type, inferred, propertyNode);
241 |     Property oldProp = properties.get(name);
242 |     if (oldProp != null) {
243 |       // This is to keep previously inferred JsDoc info, e.g., in a
244 |       // replaceScript scenario.
245 |       newProp.setJSDocInfo(oldProp.getJSDocInfo());
246 |     }
247 |     properties.put(name, newProp);
248 |     return true;
249 |   }

===== M0269 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ErrorFunctionType.ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ErrorFunctionType.java
Original range: 50-74
Documentation:

Method code:
50 |   ErrorFunctionType(JSTypeRegistry registry, String name) {
51 |     super(
52 |         registry, name, null,
53 |         registry.createArrowType(
54 |             registry.createOptionalParameters(
55 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
56 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE),
57 |                 registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE)),
58 |             null),
59 |         null, null, true, true);
60 | 
61 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Errors have the weird behavior in that they can
62 |     // be called as functions, and they will return instances of themselves.
63 |     // Error('x') instanceof Error => true
64 |     //
65 |     // In user-defined types, we would deal with this case by creating
66 |     // a NamedType with the name "Error" and then resolve it later.
67 |     //
68 |     // For native types, we don't really want the native types to
69 |     // depend on type-resolution. So we just set the return type manually
70 |     // at the end of construction.
71 |     //
72 |     // There's similar logic in JSTypeRegistry for Array and RegExp.
73 |     getInternalArrowType().returnType = getInstanceType();
74 |   }

Method code:
154 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
155 |     switch (n.getType()) {
156 |       // Childless expressions
157 |       case Token.FALSE:
158 |       case Token.NULL:
159 |       case Token.THIS:

162 |         return;
163 | 
164 |       // General unary ops
165 |       case Token.DELPROP:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.NEG:
168 |       case Token.NOT:
169 |       case Token.INC:
170 |       case Token.DEC:
171 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
172 |       case Token.VOID:
173 |       case Token.BITNOT:
174 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
175 |         return;
176 | 
177 |       // General binary ops
178 |       case Token.COMMA:
179 |       case Token.OR:
180 |       case Token.AND:
181 |       case Token.BITOR:
182 |       case Token.BITXOR:
183 |       case Token.BITAND:
184 |       case Token.EQ:
185 |       case Token.NE:
186 |       case Token.SHEQ:
187 |       case Token.SHNE:
188 |       case Token.LT:
189 |       case Token.GT:
190 |       case Token.LE:
191 |       case Token.GE:
192 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
193 |       case Token.IN:
194 |       case Token.LSH:
195 |       case Token.RSH:
196 |       case Token.URSH:
197 |       case Token.SUB:
198 |       case Token.ADD:
199 |       case Token.MUL:
200 |       case Token.MOD:
201 |       case Token.DIV:
202 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
203 |         return;
204 | 
205 |       // Assignments
206 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
216 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
217 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
218 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
219 |         return;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.HOOK:
222 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
223 |         return;
224 | 
225 |       // Node types that require special handling
226 |       case Token.STRING:
227 |         validateString(n);
228 |         return;
229 | 
230 |       case Token.NUMBER:
231 |         validateNumber(n);
232 |         return;
233 | 
234 |       case Token.NAME:
235 |         validateName(n);
236 |         return;
237 | 
238 |       case Token.GETELEM:
239 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
240 |         return;
241 | 
242 |       case Token.GETPROP:
243 |         validateGetProp(n);
244 |         return;
245 | 
246 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
247 |         validateArrayLit(n);
248 |         return;
249 | 
250 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
251 |         validateObjectLit(n);
252 |         return;
253 | 
254 |       case Token.REGEXP:
255 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
256 |         return;
257 | 
258 |       case Token.CALL:
259 |         validateCall(n);
260 |         return;
261 | 
262 |       case Token.NEW:
263 |         validateNew(n);
264 |         return;
265 | 
266 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
267 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
268 |         return;
269 | 
270 |       default:
271 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
272 |             + Token.name(n.getType()), n);
273 |     }
274 |   }

Method code:
1009 |   private static void toStringTreeHelper(Node n, int level, Appendable sb)
1010 |       throws IOException {
1011 |     for (int i = 0; i != level; ++i) {
1012 |       sb.append("    ");
1013 |     }
1014 |     sb.append(n.toString());
1015 |     sb.append('\n');
1016 |     for (Node cursor = n.getFirstChild();
1017 |          cursor != null;
1018 |          cursor = cursor.getNext()) {
1019 |       toStringTreeHelper(cursor, level + 1, sb);
1020 |     }
1021 |   }

===== M0287 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.precedence(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1168-1241
Documentation:
/*
   *  0 comma ,
   *  1 assignment = += -= *= /= %= <<= >>= >>>= &= ^= |=
   *  2 conditional ?:
   *  3 logical-or ||
   *  4 logical-and &&
   *  5 bitwise-or |
   *  6 bitwise-xor ^
   *  7 bitwise-and &
   *  8 equality == !=
   *  9 relational < <= > >=
   * 10 bitwise shift << >> >>>
   * 11 addition/subtraction + -
   * 12 multiply/divide * / %
   * 13 negation/increment ! ~ - ++ --
   * 14 call, member () [] .
   */
Method code:
1168 |   static int precedence(int type) {
1169 |     switch (type) {
1170 |       case Token.COMMA:  return 0;
1171 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
1172 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
1173 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
1174 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
1175 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
1176 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
1177 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
1178 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
1179 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
1180 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
1181 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
1182 |       case Token.ASSIGN: return 1;
1183 |       case Token.HOOK:   return 2;  // ?: operator
1184 |       case Token.OR:     return 3;
1185 |       case Token.AND:    return 4;
1186 |       case Token.BITOR:  return 5;
1187 |       case Token.BITXOR: return 6;
1188 |       case Token.BITAND: return 7;
1189 |       case Token.EQ:
1190 |       case Token.NE:
1191 |       case Token.SHEQ:
1192 |       case Token.SHNE:   return 8;
1193 |       case Token.LT:
1194 |       case Token.GT:
1195 |       case Token.LE:
1196 |       case Token.GE:
1197 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
1198 |       case Token.IN:     return 9;
1199 |       case Token.LSH:
1200 |       case Token.RSH:
1201 |       case Token.URSH:   return 10;
1202 |       case Token.SUB:
1203 |       case Token.ADD:    return 11;
1204 |       case Token.MUL:
1205 |       case Token.MOD:
1206 |       case Token.DIV:    return 12;
1207 |       case Token.INC:
1208 |       case Token.DEC:
1209 |       case Token.NEW:
1210 |       case Token.DELPROP:
1211 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
1212 |       case Token.VOID:
1213 |       case Token.NOT:
1214 |       case Token.BITNOT:
1215 |       case Token.POS:
1216 |       case Token.NEG:    return 13;
1217 | 
1218 |       case Token.CALL:
1219 |       case Token.GETELEM:
1220 |       case Token.GETPROP:
1221 |       // Data values
1222 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
1223 |       case Token.EMPTY:  // TODO(johnlenz): remove this.
1224 |       case Token.FALSE:
1225 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1226 |       case Token.NAME:
1227 |       case Token.NULL:
1228 |       case Token.NUMBER:
1229 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
1230 |       case Token.REGEXP:
1231 |       case Token.STRING:
1232 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
1233 |       case Token.THIS:
1234 |       case Token.TRUE:
1235 |         return 15;
1236 | 
1237 |       default: throw new Error("Unknown precedence for " +
1238 |                                Token.name(type) +
1239 |                                " (type " + type + ")");
1240 |     }
1241 |   }

===== M0290 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 619-631
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
619 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
620 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
621 |     if (typeSet == null) {
622 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
623 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
624 |     }
625 | 
626 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
627 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
628 | 
629 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
630 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
631 |   }

===== M0300 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 511-546
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
512 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
514 | 
515 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
516 | 
517 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
518 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
519 | 
520 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
521 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
522 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
523 |     }
524 | 
525 |     curNode = n;
526 |     pushScope(n);
527 | 
528 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
529 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
530 |       // scope.
531 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
535 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
536 | 
537 |     // Args
538 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
539 | 
540 |     // Body
541 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
542 |             body.isBlock(), body);
543 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
544 | 
545 |     popScope();
546 |   }

===== M0305 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CollapseVariableDeclarations.applyCollapses()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CollapseVariableDeclarations.java
Original range: 209-245
Documentation:

Method code:
209 |   private void applyCollapses() {
210 |     for (Collapse collapse : collapses) {
211 | 
212 |       Node var = new Node(Token.VAR);
213 |       var.copyInformationFrom(collapse.startNode);
214 |       collapse.parent.addChildBefore(var, collapse.startNode);
215 | 
216 |       boolean redeclaration = false;
217 |       for (Node n = collapse.startNode; n != collapse.endNode;) {
218 |         Node next = n.getNext();
219 | 
220 |         Preconditions.checkState(var.getNext() == n);
221 |         collapse.parent.removeChildAfter(var);
222 | 
223 |         if (n.isVar()) {
224 |           while(n.hasChildren()) {
225 |             var.addChildToBack(n.removeFirstChild());
226 |           }
227 |         } else {
228 |           Node assign = n.getFirstChild();
229 |           Node lhs = assign.getFirstChild();
230 |           Preconditions.checkState(lhs.isName());
231 |           Node rhs = assign.getLastChild();
232 |           lhs.addChildToBack(rhs.detachFromParent());
233 |           var.addChildToBack(lhs.detachFromParent());
234 |           redeclaration = true;
235 |         }
236 |         n = next;
237 |       }
238 | 
239 |       if (redeclaration) {
240 |         JSDocInfo info = new JSDocInfo();
241 |         info.addSuppression("duplicate");
242 |         var.setJSDocInfo(info);
243 |       }
244 |     }
245 |   }

Original range: 448-483
Documentation:
/** Set the prototype without doing any sanity checks. */
Method code:
448 |   private boolean setPrototypeNoCheck(ObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
449 |     ObjectType oldPrototype = prototypeSlot == null
450 |         ? null : (ObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
451 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;
452 | 
453 |     this.prototypeSlot = new Property("prototype", prototype, true,
454 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
455 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
456 | 
457 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
458 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
459 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
460 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
464 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
465 |       if (superClass != null) {
466 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
467 |       }
468 | 
469 |       if (isInterface()) {
470 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
471 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
472 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
473 |           }
474 |         }
475 |       }
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
479 |       clearCachedValues();
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     return true;
483 |   }

===== M0309 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 403-429
Documentation:

Method code:
403 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
404 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
405 |     // two cases:
406 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
407 |     // and
408 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
409 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
410 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
411 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
412 |     //
413 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
414 |     //
415 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
416 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
417 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
418 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
419 |     //
420 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
421 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
422 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
423 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
424 | 
425 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
426 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
427 |     }
428 |     setPrototype(baseType, propertyNode);
429 |   }

===== M0322 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2406-2415
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2406 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2407 |     int len = s.length();
2408 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2409 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2410 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2411 |         return false;
2412 |       }
2413 |     }
2414 |     return true;
2415 |   }
