===== M0336 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.add(Node,Context)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 85-732
Documentation:

Method code:
85 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
86 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
87 |       return;
88 |     }
89 | 
90 |     int type = n.getType();
91 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
92 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
93 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();
94 |     Node last = n.getLastChild();
95 | 
96 |     // Handle all binary operators
97 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(
99 |           childCount == 2,
100 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
101 |           opstr, childCount);
102 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
103 | 
104 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
105 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
106 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
107 | 
108 |       // Handle associativity.
109 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
110 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
111 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
112 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
113 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
114 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
117 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
118 |         addExpr(first, p, context);
119 |         cc.addOp(opstr, true);
120 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
121 |       } else {
122 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, type, opstr, context, rhsContext, p, p + 1);
123 |       }
124 |       return;
125 |     }
126 | 
127 |     cc.startSourceMapping(n);
128 | 
129 |     switch (type) {
130 |       case Token.TRY: {
131 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().isBlock() &&
132 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
133 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
134 | 
135 |         add("try");
136 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
137 | 
138 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
139 |         // isn't a catch block
140 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
141 |         if (catchblock != null) {
142 |           add(catchblock);
143 |         }
144 | 
145 |         if (childCount == 3) {
146 |           add("finally");
147 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
148 |         }
149 |         break;
150 |       }
151 | 
152 |       case Token.CATCH:
153 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
154 |         add("catch(");
155 |         add(first);
156 |         add(")");
157 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
158 |         break;
159 | 
160 |       case Token.THROW:
161 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
162 |         add("throw");
163 |         add(first);
164 | 
165 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
166 |         // parse this.
167 |         cc.endStatement(true);
168 |         break;
169 | 
170 |       case Token.RETURN:
171 |         add("return");
172 |         if (childCount == 1) {
173 |           add(first);
174 |         } else {
175 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
176 |         }
177 |         cc.endStatement();
178 |         break;
179 | 
180 |       case Token.VAR:
181 |         if (first != null) {
182 |           add("var ");
183 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
184 |         }
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
188 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
189 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NAME:
193 |         if (first == null || first.isEmpty()) {
194 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
195 |         } else {
196 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
197 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
198 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
199 |           if (first.isComma()) {
200 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN), Context.OTHER);
201 |           } else {
202 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
203 |             // precedence.
204 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
205 |           }
206 |         }
207 |         break;
208 | 
209 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
210 |         add("[");
211 |         addArrayList(first);
212 |         add("]");
213 |         break;
214 | 
215 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
216 |         add("(");
217 |         addList(first);
218 |         add(")");
219 |         break;
220 | 
221 |       case Token.COMMA:
222 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
223 |         unrollBinaryOperator(n, Token.COMMA, ",", context, Context.OTHER, 0, 0);
224 |         break;
225 | 
226 |       case Token.NUMBER:
227 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
228 |         cc.addNumber(n.getDouble());
229 |         break;
230 | 
231 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
232 |       case Token.VOID:
233 |       case Token.NOT:
234 |       case Token.BITNOT:
235 |       case Token.POS: {
236 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
237 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
238 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
239 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
240 |         break;
241 |       }
242 | 
243 |       case Token.NEG: {
244 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
245 | 
246 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
247 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
248 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
249 |         if (n.getFirstChild().isNumber()) {
250 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
251 |         } else {
252 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
253 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), Context.OTHER);
254 |         }
255 | 
256 |         break;
257 |       }
258 | 
259 |       case Token.HOOK: {
260 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
261 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
262 |         addExpr(first, p + 1, context);
263 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
264 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1, Context.OTHER);
265 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
266 |         addExpr(last, 1, Context.OTHER);
267 |         break;
268 |       }
269 | 
270 |       case Token.REGEXP:
271 |         if (!first.isString() ||
272 |             !last.isString()) {
273 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
274 |         }
275 | 
276 |         String regexp = regexpEscape(first.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder);
277 | 
278 |         // I only use one .add because whitespace matters
279 |         if (childCount == 2) {
280 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
281 |         } else {
282 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
283 |           add(regexp);
284 |         }
285 |         break;
286 | 
287 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
288 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
289 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
290 |         }
291 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
292 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
293 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
294 |           add("(");
295 |         }
296 | 
297 |         add("function");
298 |         add(first);
299 | 
300 |         add(first.getNext());
301 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
302 |         cc.endFunction(context == Context.STATEMENT);
303 | 
304 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
305 |           add(")");
306 |         }
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
310 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
311 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getParent().isObjectLit());
312 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
313 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.isFunction());
314 | 
315 |         // Get methods are unnamed
316 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getFirstChild().getString().isEmpty());
317 |         if (type == Token.GETTER_DEF) {
318 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
319 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
320 |           add("get ");
321 |         } else {
322 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
323 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
324 |           add("set ");
325 |         }
326 | 
327 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
328 |         String name = n.getString();
329 |         Node fn = first;
330 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
331 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
332 | 
333 |         // Add the property name.
334 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
335 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
336 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
337 |             // Unicode escaped.
338 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
339 |           add(name);
340 |         } else {
341 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
342 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
343 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
344 |             cc.addNumber(d);
345 |           } else {
346 |             addJsString(n);
347 |           }
348 |         }
349 | 
350 |         add(parameters);
351 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
352 |         break;
353 | 
354 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
355 |       case Token.BLOCK: {
356 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
357 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
358 |         }
359 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
360 |         if (preserveBlock) {
361 |           cc.beginBlock();
362 |         }
363 | 
364 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
365 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||
366 |             (type == Token.BLOCK &&
367 |                 !preserveBlock &&
368 |                 n.getParent() != null &&
369 |                 n.getParent().isScript());
370 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
371 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);
372 | 
373 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
374 |           if (c.isVar()) {
375 |             cc.endStatement();
376 |           }
377 | 
378 |           if (c.isFunction()) {
379 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
380 |           }
381 | 
382 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
383 |           // because top-level statements are more homogeneous.
384 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {
385 |             cc.notePreferredLineBreak();
386 |           }
387 |         }
388 |         if (preserveBlock) {
389 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
390 |         }
391 |         break;
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       case Token.FOR:
395 |         if (childCount == 4) {
396 |           add("for(");
397 |           if (first.isVar()) {
398 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
399 |           } else {
400 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
401 |           }
402 |           add(";");
403 |           add(first.getNext());
404 |           add(";");
405 |           add(first.getNext().getNext());
406 |           add(")");
407 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
408 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
409 |         } else {
410 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
411 |           add("for(");
412 |           add(first);
413 |           add("in");
414 |           add(first.getNext());
415 |           add(")");
416 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
417 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
418 |         }
419 |         break;
420 | 
421 |       case Token.DO:
422 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
423 |         add("do");
424 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
425 |         add("while(");
426 |         add(last);
427 |         add(")");
428 |         cc.endStatement();
429 |         break;
430 | 
431 |       case Token.WHILE:
432 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
433 |         add("while(");
434 |         add(first);
435 |         add(")");
436 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
437 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
438 |         break;
439 | 
440 |       case Token.EMPTY:
441 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
442 |         break;
443 | 
444 |       case Token.GETPROP: {
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(
446 |             childCount == 2,
447 |             "Bad GETPROP: expected 2 children, but got %s", childCount);
448 |         Preconditions.checkState(
449 |             last.isString(),
450 |             "Bad GETPROP: RHS should be STRING");
451 |         boolean needsParens = (first.isNumber());
452 |         if (needsParens) {
453 |           add("(");
454 |         }
455 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
456 |         if (needsParens) {
457 |           add(")");
458 |         }
459 |         add(".");
460 |         addIdentifier(last.getString());
461 |         break;
462 |       }
463 | 
464 |       case Token.GETELEM:
465 |         Preconditions.checkState(
466 |             childCount == 2,
467 |             "Bad GETELEM: expected 2 children but got %s", childCount);
468 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
469 |         add("[");
470 |         add(first.getNext());
471 |         add("]");
472 |         break;
473 | 
474 |       case Token.WITH:
475 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
476 |         add("with(");
477 |         add(first);
478 |         add(")");
479 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
480 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.INC:
484 |       case Token.DEC: {
485 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
486 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
487 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
488 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
489 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
490 |         if (postProp != 0) {
491 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
492 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
493 |         } else {
494 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
495 |           add(first);
496 |         }
497 |         break;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       case Token.CALL:
501 |         // We have two special cases here:
502 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
503 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
504 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
505 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
506 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
507 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
508 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
509 |         // that must be preserved.
510 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
511 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
512 |           add("(0,");
513 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA), Context.OTHER);
514 |           add(")");
515 |         } else {
516 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
517 |         }
518 |         add("(");
519 |         addList(first.getNext());
520 |         add(")");
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.IF:
524 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
525 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
526 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
527 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
528 |           cc.beginBlock();
529 |         }
530 | 
531 |         add("if(");
532 |         add(first);
533 |         add(")");
534 | 
535 |         if (hasElse) {
536 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
537 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
538 |           add("else");
539 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
540 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
541 |         } else {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
543 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
544 |         }
545 | 
546 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
547 |           cc.endBlock();
548 |         }
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.NULL:
552 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
553 |         cc.addConstant("null");
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.THIS:
557 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
558 |         add("this");
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.FALSE:
562 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
563 |         cc.addConstant("false");
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TRUE:
567 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
568 |         cc.addConstant("true");
569 |         break;
570 | 
571 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
572 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
573 |         add("continue");
574 |         if (childCount == 1) {
575 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
576 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
577 |           }
578 |           add(" ");
579 |           add(first);
580 |         }
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
586 |         add("debugger");
587 |         cc.endStatement();
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.BREAK:
591 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
592 |         add("break");
593 |         if (childCount == 1) {
594 |           if (!first.isLabelName()) {
595 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
596 |           }
597 |           add(" ");
598 |           add(first);
599 |         }
600 |         cc.endStatement();
601 |         break;
602 | 
603 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
604 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
605 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
606 |         cc.endStatement();
607 |         break;
608 | 
609 |       case Token.NEW:
610 |         add("new ");
611 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
612 | 
613 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
614 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
615 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
616 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(
617 |             first, Token.CALL, NodeUtil.MATCH_NOT_FUNCTION)) {
618 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
619 |         }
620 |         addExpr(first, precedence, Context.OTHER);
621 | 
622 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
623 |         Node next = first.getNext();
624 |         if (next != null) {
625 |           add("(");
626 |           addList(next);
627 |           add(")");
628 |         }
629 |         break;
630 | 
631 |       case Token.STRING_KEY:
632 |         Preconditions.checkState(
633 |             childCount == 1, "Object lit key must have 1 child");
634 |         addJsString(n);
635 |         break;
636 | 
637 |       case Token.STRING:
638 |         Preconditions.checkState(
639 |             childCount == 0, "A string may not have children");
640 |         addJsString(n);
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.DELPROP:
644 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
645 |         add("delete ");
646 |         add(first);
647 |         break;
648 | 
649 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
650 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
651 |         if (needsParens) {
652 |           add("(");
653 |         }
654 |         add("{");
655 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
656 |           if (c != first) {
657 |             cc.listSeparator();
658 |           }
659 | 
660 |           if (c.isGetterDef() || c.isSetterDef()) {
661 |             add(c);
662 |           } else {
663 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.isStringKey());
664 |             String key = c.getString();
665 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
666 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
667 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
668 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
669 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
670 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
671 |                 // were Unicode escaped.
672 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
673 |               add(key);
674 |             } else {
675 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
676 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
677 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
678 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
679 |               } else {
680 |                 addExpr(c, 1, Context.OTHER);
681 |               }
682 |             }
683 |             add(":");
684 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1, Context.OTHER);
685 |           }
686 |         }
687 |         add("}");
688 |         if (needsParens) {
689 |           add(")");
690 |         }
691 |         break;
692 |       }
693 | 
694 |       case Token.SWITCH:
695 |         add("switch(");
696 |         add(first);
697 |         add(")");
698 |         cc.beginBlock();
699 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
700 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
701 |         break;
702 | 
703 |       case Token.CASE:
704 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
705 |         add("case ");
706 |         add(first);
707 |         addCaseBody(last);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
711 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
712 |         add("default");
713 |         addCaseBody(first);
714 |         break;
715 | 
716 |       case Token.LABEL:
717 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
718 |         if (!first.isLabelName()) {
719 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
720 |         }
721 |         add(first);
722 |         add(":");
723 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
724 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
725 |         break;
726 | 
727 |       default:
728 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
729 |     }
730 | 
731 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
732 |   }

===== M0339 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 929-988
Documentation:

Method code:
929 |   private void toString(
930 |       StringBuilder sb,
931 |       boolean printSource,
932 |       boolean printAnnotations,
933 |       boolean printType) {
934 |     sb.append(Token.name(type));
935 |     if (this instanceof StringNode) {
936 |       sb.append(' ');
937 |       sb.append(getString());
938 |     } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
939 |       sb.append(' ');
940 |       // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
941 |       // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
942 |       // toStringTree.
943 |       if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
944 |         sb.append("<invalid>");
945 |       } else {
946 |         sb.append(first.getString());
947 |       }
948 |     } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
949 |       sb.append(' ');
950 |       sb.append(getDouble());
951 |     }
952 |     if (printSource) {
953 |       int lineno = getLineno();
954 |       if (lineno != -1) {
955 |         sb.append(' ');
956 |         sb.append(lineno);
957 |       }
958 |     }
959 | 
960 |     if (printAnnotations) {
961 |       int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
962 |       for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
963 |         int type = keys[i];
964 |         PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
965 |         sb.append(" [");
966 |         sb.append(propToString(type));
967 |         sb.append(": ");
968 |         String value;
969 |         switch (type) {
970 |           default:
971 |             value = x.toString();
972 |             break;
973 |         }
974 |         sb.append(value);
975 |         sb.append(']');
976 |       }
977 |     }
978 | 
979 |     if (printType) {
980 |       if (jsType != null) {
981 |         String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
982 |         if (jsTypeString != null) {
983 |           sb.append(" : ");
984 |           sb.append(jsTypeString);
985 |         }
986 |       }
987 |     }
988 |   }

===== M0349 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1926-1928
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1926 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1927 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1928 |   }

176 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,

184 |

191 |           irFactory.handleBlockComment(comment);

199 |   }

Method code:
859 |   private int[] getSortedPropTypes() {
860 |     int count = 0;
861 |     for (PropListItem x = propListHead; x != null; x = x.getNext()) {
862 |       count++;
863 |     }
864 |

789 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {
790 |     boolean valid = false;
791 |     if (i == 1) {
792 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
793 |     } else if (i == 2) {
794 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
795 |     } else {
796 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
797 |     }
798 | 
799 |     if (!valid) {
800 |       violation(
801 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
802 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
803 |     }
804 |   }

633 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
634 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
635 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
636 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
637 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
638 |       if (typeSet == null) {
639 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
640 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
641 |       }
642 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
643 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
644 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
645 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
646 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
647 |     } else if (type.isUnionType()) {
648 |       for (JSType alternate : type.toMaybeUnionType().getAlternates()) {
649 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
650 |       }
651 |     }
652 |   }

===== M0386 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 477-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
477 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
478 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
479 |     int type = n.getType();
480 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
481 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
482 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     curNode = n;
486 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
487 | 
488 |     switch (type) {
489 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
490 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
491 |         break;
492 | 
493 |       default:
494 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
495 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
496 |           // would no longer point to the true next
497 |           Node next = child.getNext();
498 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
499 |           child = next;
500 |         }
501 |         break;
502 |     }
503 | 
504 |     curNode = n;
505 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
506 |   }

===== M0388 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.DefaultRedeclarationHandler.onRedeclaration(Scope,String,Node,CompilerInput)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 192-225
Documentation:

Method code:
192 |     @Override
193 |     public void onRedeclaration(
194 |         Scope s, String name, Node n, CompilerInput input) {
195 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
196 | 
197 |       // Don't allow multiple variables to be declared at the top-level scope
198 |       if (scope.isGlobal()) {
199 |         Scope.Var origVar = scope.getVar(name);
200 |         Node origParent = origVar.getParentNode();
201 |         if (origParent.isCatch() &&
202 |             parent.isCatch()) {
203 |           // Okay, both are 'catch(x)' variables.
204 |           return;
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         boolean allowDupe = hasDuplicateDeclarationSuppression(n, origVar);
208 | 
209 |         if (!allowDupe) {
210 |           compiler.report(
211 |               JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
212 |                            VAR_MULTIPLY_DECLARED_ERROR,
213 |                            name,
214 |                            (origVar.input != null
215 |                             ? origVar.input.getName()
216 |                             : "??")));
217 |         }
218 |       } else if (name.equals(ARGUMENTS) && !NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n)) {
219 |         // Disallow shadowing "arguments" as we can't handle with our current
220 |         // scope modeling.
221 |         compiler.report(
222 |             JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
223 |                 VAR_ARGUMENTS_SHADOWED_ERROR));
224 |       }
225 |     }

516 |       if (!preferLineBreakAtEndOfFile) {
517 |         return;
518 |       }
519 |       if (lineLength > lineLengthThreshold / 2) {
520 |         // Add an extra break at end of file.
521 |         append(";");
522 |         startNewLine();
523 |       } else if (prevCutPosition > 0) {
524 |         // Shift the previous break to end of file by replacing it with a
525 |         // <space> and adding a new break at end of file. Adding the space
526 |         // handles cases like instanceof\nfoo. (it would be nice to avoid this)
527 |         code.setCharAt(prevCutPosition, ' ');
528 |         lineStartPosition = prevLineStartPosition;
529 |         lineLength = code.length() - lineStartPosition;
530 |         reportLineCut(lineIndex, prevCutPosition + 1, false);
531 |         lineIndex--;
532 |         prevCutPosition = 0;
533 |         prevLineStartPosition = 0;
534 |         append(";");
535 |         startNewLine();
536 |       } else {
537 |         // A small file with no line breaks. We do nothing in this case to
538 |         // avoid excessive line breaks. It's not ideal if a lot of these pile
539 |         // up, but that is reasonably unlikely.
540 |       }
541 |     }

===== M0408 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.add(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 181-204
Documentation:

Method code:
181 |   void add(String newcode) {
182 |     maybeEndStatement();
183 | 
184 |     if (newcode.length() == 0) {
185 |       return;
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     char c = newcode.charAt(0);
189 |     if ((isWordChar(c) || c == '\\') &&
190 |         isWordChar(getLastChar())) {
191 |       // need space to separate. This is not pretty printing.
192 |       // For example: "return foo;"
193 |       append(" ");
194 |     } else if (c == '/' && getLastChar() == '/') {
195 |       // Do not allow a forward slash to appear after a DIV.
196 |       // For example,
197 |       // REGEXP DIV REGEXP
198 |       // is valid and should print like
199 |       // / // / /
200 |       append(" ");
201 |     }
202 | 
203 |     append(newcode);
204 |   }

===== M0409 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1280-1402
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1280 |   Node parseInputs() {
1281 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1282 | 
1283 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1284 |     // individual file parse trees.
1285 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1286 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1287 |     }
1288 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1289 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1290 |     }
1291 | 
1292 |     // Parse main JS sources.
1293 |     jsRoot = IR.block();
1294 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1295 | 
1296 |     externsRoot = IR.block();
1297 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1298 | 
1299 |     externAndJsRoot = IR.block(externsRoot, jsRoot);
1300 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1301 | 
1302 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1303 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot, options.tracer);
1304 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1305 |     }
1306 | 
1307 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1308 | 
1309 |     try {
1310 |       // Parse externs sources.
1311 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1312 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1313 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1314 |           return null;
1315 |         }
1316 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1317 |       }
1318 | 
1319 |       // Modules inferred in ProcessCommonJS pass.
1320 |       if (options.transformAMDToCJSModules || options.processCommonJSModules) {
1321 |         processAMDAndCommonJSModules();
1322 |       }
1323 | 
1324 |       hoistExterns(externsRoot);
1325 | 
1326 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1327 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1328 |       if (options.dependencyOptions.needsManagement()) {
1329 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1330 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1331 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1332 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1333 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1334 |           }
1335 |         }
1336 | 
1337 |         try {
1338 |           inputs =
1339 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1340 |               .manageDependencies(options.dependencyOptions, inputs);
1341 |           staleInputs = true;
1342 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1343 |           report(JSError.make(
1344 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1345 | 
1346 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1347 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1348 |             return null;
1349 |           }
1350 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1351 |           report(JSError.make(
1352 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1353 | 
1354 |           // If in IDE mode, we ignore the error and keep going.
1355 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1356 |             return null;
1357 |           }
1358 |         }
1359 |       }
1360 | 
1361 |       hoistNoCompileFiles();
1362 | 
1363 |       if (staleInputs) {
1364 |         repartitionInputs();
1365 |       }
1366 | 
1367 |       // Build the AST.
1368 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1369 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1370 |         if (n == null) {
1371 |           continue;
1372 |         }
1373 | 
1374 |         if (devMode) {
1375 |           runSanityCheck();
1376 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1377 |             return null;
1378 |           }
1379 |         }
1380 | 
1381 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1382 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1383 | 
1384 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1385 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1386 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1387 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1388 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1389 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1390 |         }
1391 | 
1392 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1393 |       }
1394 | 
1395 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1396 |         return null;
1397 |       }
1398 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1399 |     } finally {
1400 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1401 |     }
1402 |   }

/**

   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totaling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
235 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
236 |     this.type = type;
237 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
238 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
239 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
240 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
241 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
242 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
243 |       int i = 0;
244 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
245 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
246 |         i++;
247 |       }
248 |     }
249 | 
250 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
251 | 
252 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
253 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
254 |       return;
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
258 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
259 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
260 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
261 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
262 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
263 |       trace.truncateEvents();
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
267 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
268 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
269 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
270 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
271 |                     + "try/finally block. "
272 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
273 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
274 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
275 |     }
276 | 
277 |     trace.startEvent(this);
278 |   }

/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;

===== M0440 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addOp(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 210-238
Documentation:

Method code:
210 |   void addOp(String op, boolean binOp) {
211 |     maybeEndStatement();
212 | 
213 |     char first = op.charAt(0);
214 |     char prev = getLastChar();
215 | 
216 |     if ((first == '+' || first == '-') && prev == first) {
217 |       // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of
218 |       // things like "x + ++y" or "x++ + ++y"
219 |       append(" ");
220 |     } else if (Character.isLetter(first) &&
221 |                isWordChar(prev)) {
222 |       // Make sure there is a space after e.g. instanceof , typeof
223 |       append(" ");
224 |     } else if (prev == '-' && first == '>') {
225 |       // Make sure that we don't emit -->
226 |       append(" ");
227 |     }
228 | 
229 |     // Allow formatting around the operator.
230 |     appendOp(op, binOp);
231 | 
232 |     // Line breaking after an operator is always safe. Line breaking before an
233 |     // operator on the other hand is not. We only line break after a bin op
234 |     // because it looks strange.
235 |     if (binOp) {
236 |       maybeCutLine();
237 |     }
238 |   }

Method code:
83 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler) {
84 |     try {
85 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceFile.getName());
86 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceFile, sourceFile.getCode(),
87 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
88 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
89 |           logger_);
90 |     } catch (IOException e) {
91 |       compiler.report(
92 |           JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceFile.getName()));
93 |     }

96 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
97 |       root = IR.script();
98 |     } else {
99 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
103 |     // the source file and module.
104 |     root.setStaticSourceFile(sourceFile);
105 |   }

===== M0462 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 626-708
Documentation:

Method code:
626 |     @Override
627 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
628 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
629 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
630 |       if (name == null) {
631 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
632 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
633 |           errorReporter.error(
634 |             "unnamed function statement",
635 |             sourceName,
636 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
637 | 
638 |           // Return the bare minimum to put the AST in a valid state.
639 |           return newNode(Token.EXPR_RESULT, Node.newNumber(0));
640 |         }
641 |         name = new Name();
642 |         name.setIdentifier("");
643 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
644 |       }
645 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
646 |       Node newName = transform(name);
647 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
648 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
649 |         // declaration.
650 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
651 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
652 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
653 |         // same line as the function keyword.
654 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
655 |             functionNode.getLp();
656 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
657 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
658 |       }
659 | 
660 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
661 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
662 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
663 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
664 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
665 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
666 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
667 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
668 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
669 |       if (fnName != null) {
670 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
671 |       } else {
672 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
673 |       }
674 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
675 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
676 | 
677 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
678 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
679 |         Node paramNode = transform(param);
680 |         // When in ideMode Rhino can generate a param list with only a single
681 |         // ErrorNode. This is transformed into an EMPTY node. Drop this node in
682 |         // ideMode to keep the AST in a valid state.
683 |         if (paramNode.isName()) {
684 |           lp.addChildToBack(paramNode);
685 |         } else {
686 |           // We expect this in ideMode or when there is an error handling
687 |           // destructuring parameter assignments which aren't supported
688 |           // (an error has already been reported).
689 |           Preconditions.checkState(
690 |               config.isIdeMode
691 |               || paramNode.isObjectLit()
692 |               || paramNode.isArrayLit());
693 |         }
694 |       }
695 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
696 | 
697 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
698 |       if (!bodyNode.isBlock()) {
699 |         // When in ideMode Rhino tries to parse some constructs the compiler
700 |         // doesn't support, repair it here. see Rhino's
701 |         // Parser#parseFunctionBodyExpr.
702 |         Preconditions.checkState(config.isIdeMode);
703 |         bodyNode = IR.block();
704 |       }
705 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
706 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
707 |      return node;
708 |     }

/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 | 
117 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
118 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
119 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
120 | 
121 |     // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |     boolean acceptEs5 =
123 |         config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3;
124 |     compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(acceptEs5);
125 | 
126 |     compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(false);
127 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
128 |     compilerEnv.setRecoverFromErrors(config.isIdeMode);
129 | 
130 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
131 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
132 |     try {
133 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
134 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
135 |       logger.info(
136 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
137 |     } finally {
138 |       Context.exit();
139 |     }
140 |     Node root = null;
141 |     if (astRoot != null) {
142 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
143 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
144 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
145 |     }
146 |     return root;
147 |   }

===== M0470 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1104-1124
Documentation:

Method code:
1104 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1105 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1106 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1107 |       return s;
1108 |     }
1109 | 
1110 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-Latin characters if needed.
1111 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1112 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1113 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1114 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1115 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1116 |       // crazy.
1117 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1118 |         sb.append(c);
1119 |       } else {
1120 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1121 |       }
1122 |     }
1123 |     return sb.toString();
1124 |   }

===== M0477 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-214
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarre sub-lattice. See the comments on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType() ||
176 |               alternate.hasAnyTemplate() ||
177 |               current.hasAnyTemplate()) {
178 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
179 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
180 |               return this;
181 |             }
182 |           } else {
183 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
184 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
185 |               return this;
186 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
187 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
188 |               it.remove();
189 | 
190 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
192 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
193 |                 functionTypePosition--;
194 |                 currentIndex--;
195 |               }
196 |             }
197 |           }
198 |           currentIndex++;
199 |         }
200 | 
201 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
202 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
203 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
204 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
205 |         }
206 | 
207 |         alternates.add(alternate);
208 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
209 |       }
210 |     } else {
211 |       result = null;
212 |     }
213 |     return this;
214 |   }

===== M0516 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1176-1184
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1176 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1177 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1178 |       return -1;
1179 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1180 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1181 |     } else {
1182 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1183 |     }
1184 |   }

92 |   public void validateStatement(Node n) {

97 |       case Token.BLOCK:

99 |         return;
100 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
101 |         validateFunctionStatement(n);
102 |         return;
103 |       case Token.WITH:
104 |         validateWith(n);
105 |         return;
106 |       case Token.FOR:
107 |         validateFor(n);
108 |         return;
109 |       case Token.WHILE:
110 |         validateWhile(n);
111 |         return;
112 |       case Token.DO:
113 |         validateDo(n);
114 |         return;
115 |       case Token.SWITCH:
116 |         validateSwitch(n);
117 |         return;
118 |       case Token.IF:
119 |         validateIf(n);
120 |         return;
121 |       case Token.VAR:
122 |         validateVar(n);
123 |         return;
124 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
125 |         validateExprStmt(n);
126 |         return;
127 |       case Token.RETURN:
128 |         validateReturn(n);
129 |         return;
130 |       case Token.THROW:
131 |         validateThrow(n);
132 |         return;
133 |       case Token.TRY:
134 |         validateTry(n);
135 |         return;
136 |       case Token.BREAK:

138 |         return;
139 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
140 |         validateContinue(n);
141 |         return;
142 |       case Token.EMPTY:
143 |         validateChildless(n);
144 |         return;
145 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
146 |         validateChildless(n);
147 |         return;
148 |       default:

150 |             + Token.name(n.getType()) + ".", n);
151 |     }

===== M0525 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.position2charno(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 376-384
Documentation:

Method code:
376 |   private int position2charno(int position) {
377 |     int lineIndex = sourceString.lastIndexOf('\n', position);
378 |     if (lineIndex == -1) {
379 |       return position;

===== M0537 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-490
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     FunctionType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 | 
467 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     } else if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
474 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
475 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
476 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
477 |           return true;
478 |         }
479 |       }
480 |     }
481 | 
482 |     // other prototype based objects
483 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
484 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
485 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
486 |       // to avoid guessing.
487 |       return true;
488 |     }
489 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
490 |   }

===== M0543 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1425-1444
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1425 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1426 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1427 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1428 |     }
1429 | 
1430 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1431 |     Node n, n2;
1432 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1433 |          res == null && n != null;
1434 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1435 |       if (node2 == null) {
1436 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1437 |       }
1438 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1439 |       if (res != null) {
1440 |         return res;
1441 |       }
1442 |     }
1443 |     return res;
1444 |   }

===== M0551 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1081-1083
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
1081 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
1082 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
1083 |   }

===== M0581 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.getAllInputsFromModules(List<JSModule>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 490-506
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
490 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
491 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
492 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
493 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
494 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
495 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
496 |         String inputName = input.getName();
497 | 
498 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
499 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
500 |         // will get an error down the line.
501 |         inputs.add(input);
502 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
503 |       }
504 |     }
505 |     return inputs;
506 |   }

===== M0583 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.IR.mayBeStatementNoReturn(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/IR.java
Original range: 499-528
Documentation:
/**
   * It isn't possible to always determine if a detached node is a expression,
   * so make a best guess.
   */
Method code:
499 |   private static boolean mayBeStatementNoReturn(Node n) {
500 |     switch (n.getType()) {
501 |       case Token.EMPTY:
502 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
503 |         // EMPTY and FUNCTION are used both in expression and statement
504 |         // contexts
505 |         return true;
506 | 
507 |       case Token.BLOCK:
508 |       case Token.BREAK:
509 |       case Token.CONST:
510 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
511 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
512 |       case Token.DO:
513 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
514 |       case Token.FOR:
515 |       case Token.IF:
516 |       case Token.LABEL:
517 |       case Token.SWITCH:
518 |       case Token.THROW:
519 |       case Token.TRY:
520 |       case Token.VAR:
521 |       case Token.WHILE:
522 |       case Token.WITH:
523 |         return true;
524 | 
525 |       default:
526 |         return false;
527 |     }
528 |   }

===== M0590 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 240-276
Documentation:

Method code:
240 |   void addNumber(double x) {
241 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
242 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
243 |     char prev = getLastChar();
244 |     boolean negativeZero = isNegativeZero(x);
245 |     if ((x < 0 || negativeZero) && prev == '-') {
246 |       add(" ");
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     if (negativeZero) {
250 |       addConstant("-0");
251 |     } else if ((long) x == x) {
252 |       long value = (long) x;
253 |       long mantissa = value;
254 |       int exp = 0;
255 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
256 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
257 |           mantissa /= 10;
258 |           exp++;
259 |         }
260 |       }
261 |       if (exp > 2) {
262 |         addConstant(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
263 |       } else {
264 |         long valueAbs = Math.abs(value);
265 |         if (Long.toHexString(valueAbs).length() + 2 <
266 |             Long.toString(valueAbs).length()) {
267 |           addConstant((value < 0 ? "-" : "") + "0x" +
268 |               Long.toHexString(valueAbs));
269 |         } else {
270 |           addConstant(Long.toString(value));
271 |         }
272 |       }
273 |     } else {
274 |       addConstant(String.valueOf(x).replace(".0E", "E"));
275 |     }
276 |   }

===== M0593 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Scope.isDeclared(String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Scope.java
Original range: 558-567
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if a variable is declared.
   */
Method code:
558 |   public boolean isDeclared(String name, boolean recurse) {
559 |     Scope scope = this;
560 |     if (scope.vars.containsKey(name))
561 |       return true;
562 | 
563 |     if (scope.parent != null && recurse) {
564 |       return scope.parent.isDeclared(name, recurse);
565 |     }
566 |     return false;
567 |   }
